# FIXES REPORT — to-review.csv (PRU-15 2022 Sarawak)

**Original**: `git show 6fb5fde` (pre-review)
**Fixed**: current `to-review.csv`
**Total rows changed**: 2761 out of 4317 data rows

---

## Summary of Fix Categories

| # | Category | Rows Affected | Description |
|---|----------|--------------|-------------|
| 1 | Postal Vote Format | 31 | UNIQUE CODE, STATE CONSTITUENCY CODE, POLLING DISTRICT CODE corrected to `P.XXX_POS_1` / `XXX/POS` pattern |
| 2 | UNIQUE CODE Suffix | 2715 | Rebuilt all codes with correct `_CHANNELletter` suffix algorithm per Polling District Code |
| 3 | BA'KELALAN Typo | 32 | Backtick (0x60) replaced with apostrophe (0x27) in STATE CONSTITUENCY NAME for N.81 |
| 4 | Candidate Name | 113 | `ZULBAIDAH SUBOH` → `ZULHAIDAH SUBOH` in P.218 SIBUTI (verified against official PDF) |
| 5 | Comma-Formatted Number | 2 | Removed thousands separator from TOTAL VALID VOTES (`"1,616"` → `1616`, `"1,488"` → `1488`) |
| 6 | Polling Centre Correction | 1 | Corrected centre name from PDF verification (district 198/20/33) |
| 7 | Channel Number Correction | 1 | Corrected VOTING CHANNEL NUMBER from PDF verification (district 213/58/08) |
| | **TOTAL** | **2761** | |

### 2. UNIQUE CODE Suffix Algorithm (2715 rows)

All UNIQUE CODEs were rebuilt from component columns using the correct suffix algorithm:

- **Format**: `P.XXX_N.YY_PDC_CHANNELletter` (e.g., `_1a`, `_2b`)
- Suffix letter assigned per Polling District Code based on first-appearance order of Polling Centres
- Same Polling Centre → same letter across all channels
- Single-centre districts get no suffix
